```
{
  "generation_parameters": {
    "global_metadata": {
      "aspect_ratio": "3:4",
      "style_preset": "Luxury Fashion Photography",
      "aesthetic_target": "Douyin-Influencer / Modern Editorial",
      "color_profile": "High Saturation / Vivid"
    },
    "subject": {
      "identity_fidelity": {
        "primary_likeness": "{argument name="celebrity likeness" default="Ana de Armas"}",
        "constraints": "Strictly preserve original facial features; no AI-morphing"
      },
      "physique": {
        "pose": "Seated sideways on a stone balustrade; diagonal orientation",
        "modifications": "Enhanced bust silhouette; deep V-neckline focus"
      },
      "cosmetics_douyin_style": {
        "skin": "Luminous 'glass skin'; localized high-shine highlighter",
        "eyes": "Pinkish-red eyeshadow; individual manga-style lash clusters",
        "lips": "High-gloss cherry-red finish; defined borders"
      },
      "hairstyling": {
        "base": "Long, straight auburn hair; high-gloss finish",
        "arrangement": "Half-up ponytail; dual face-framing strands",
        "accents": "Gold-toned hair clips; delicate metallic beads"
      }
    },
    "apparel_system": {
      "primary_garment": {
        "item": "Sleeveless mini dress",
        "color": "{argument name="dress color" default="Deep Burgundy"}",
        "material": "Velvet with subtle leopard-print texture",
        "architecture": {
          "top": "Halter neck; deep V-plunge; open back",
          "bottom": "Layered ruffled hem; high-volume texture"
        }
      },
      "footwear": {
        "item": "Knee-high boots",
        "color": "Matching Burgundy",
        "finish": "Sleek/Polished"
      }
    },
    "environment_architecture": {
      "setting": "European Classical Villa Terrace",
      "foreground": "Weathered stone balustrade (diagonal)",
      "midground": "Old stone castle masonry (right-side frame)",
      "background": {
        "landscape": "Green valley panorama; mist-covered mountain range",
        "foliage": "Manicured lawn; flowering garden shrubs"
      }
    },
    "optical_settings": {
      "camera_angle": "Eye-level; slight side-profile",
      "framing": "Medium shot (head to knees)",

      "lens_characteristics": "Shallow depth of field; circular bokeh; sharp subject-to-background separation"

}

## Couture Runway Fashion Editorial of Hande Ercel

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A high-fashion female model walking confidently on a professional runway during a live fashion show. She holds a powerful, editorial pose with arms lifted outward in a dramatic, sculptural stance, shoulders back, chin slightly raised, and a fierce, focused runway expression.
She wears an elegant couture evening gown with a deep thigh-high slit and a structured, asymmetric upper design. The dress features flowing cape-like fabric panels extending from the shoulders/arms that drape gracefully as she moves. The fabric is premium satin/silk blend with a soft sheen that reacts beautifully to the runway lighting. The silhouette is tailored, hugging the waist and hips before flowing into a long, smooth train that glides over the glossy runway floor.
Styling includes:
Sleek high ponytail / elegant updo hairstyle
Defined cheekbones, sharp jawline makeup, bold eyes, matte lips
Minimal metallic wrist accessories / cuffs
High heels matching the gown color
Runway environment:
Bright white runway floor with glossy reflections
Audience seated on both sides in dim light
Photographers and guests blurred in the background
Occasional camera flashes
Large, dark fashion hall with overhead spotlights
Warm golden stage lights in some angles, cool white lights in others
Light atmospheric haze enhancing the beams of light
Lighting:
Strong overhead runway spotlights creating sharp highlights on shoulders and hair
Side lighting sculpting the dress folds and body contours
High contrast between illuminated runway and dark audience area
Camera & visual style:
Eye-level fashion photography angle
85mm lens look, shallow depth of field
Subject ultra-sharp, background cinematic blur (bokeh)
HDR lighting, realistic skin texture, no over-smoothing
Editorial, cinematic, magazine-grade realism
Capturing fabric physics and motion mid-walk
Mood & vibe: Elegant, powerful, couture, dramatic, confident, luxury fashion editorial moment.
Resolution: Ultra HD 8K
Aspect ratio: 4:5
Quality tags: ultra-realistic, cinematic editorial, studio-grade sharpness, professional runway photography
```

\

\
Wardrobe: ceremonial pure white kimono, matte silk texture, long sleeves forming graphic negative space. Subtle snow accumulation on fabric.\
\
Action: katana raised overhead in one hand, blade angled to catch a narrow moonlight specular; black lacquered scabbard held across torso. Perfect weapon geometry.\
\
Camera & Lens: anamorphic cinema lens feel, 65mm equivalent, slight barrel compression, shallow-to-medium depth of field, gentle anamorphic edge falloff.\
\
Composition: full-body heroic framing, slightly low angle, strong vertical symmetry, clear separation from background.\
\
Lighting: cold top-back moonlight as key, very soft fill from snow bounce, thin rim light outlining shoulders and sleeves, volumetric light through falling snow.\
\
Tone: restrained, ceremonial, lethal elegance. Premium feature-film realism. NO TEXT."

The Realism: The person is NOT a plastic figure; they have visible skin texture, natural hair, and realistic clothing folds. Every object inside has sharp, crisp, and high-contrast textures. THEIR LEGS ARE CLAD IN LIGHT BROWN OR BEIGE PANTS, INTRICATELY OUTLINED WITH A VIBRANT ORANGE-RED NEON GLOW THAT ALSO EMITS SMALL, FLICKERING NEON WISPS AROUND THEM, SUGGESTING ENERGY. THEIR FEET ARE ADORNED WITH BRIGHT PINK NEON-OUTLINED SNEAKERS, WHICH MATCH THE GLOWING PINK OUTLINE OF THE SKATEBOARD DECK BENEATH THEM. THE BACKGROUND IS AN URBAN STREET SCENE, RENDERED WITH A SHALLOW DEPTH OF FIELD, CREATING A BEAUTIFUL BOKEH EFFECT OF WARM, OUT-OF-FOCUS ORANGE AND YELLOW STRING LIGHTS STRUNG ACROSS BUILDINGS, SUGGESTING A FESTIVE OR LIVELY ATMOSPHERE. SILHOUETTES OF BLURRED PEDESTRIANS CAN BE SEEN IN THE DISTANCE. THE OVERALL LIGHTING IS CINEMATIC, WITH A SOFT GOLDEN HOUR GLOW FROM THE BACKGROUND CONTRASTING WITH THE VIBRANT. VOLUMETRIC NEON LIGHTING EMANATING FROM THE SUBJECT AND THEIR ACCESSORIES, CREATING HIGHLY

DETAILED TEXTURES AND A CAPTIVATING VISUAL.

## Cinematic Street Portrait with Motion Blur Effect

```
Ultra-realistic cinematic street portrait of a young woman standing still in a crowded city street, sharp focus on her face with calm, intense expression. Long-exposure effect with people moving around her creating strong directional motion blur, streaked crowd movement while the subject remains perfectly still. Natural soft daylight, shallow depth of field, creamy bokeh, realistic skin texture, subtle freckles, neutral makeup, dark winter coat and scarf. Emotional, introspective mood, urban storytelling photography, DSLR quality, 85mm lens look, f/1.8, high dynamic range, 8K, professional color grading.
```

## Cinematic Food Ad of Chocolate Crepes

```
Cinematic food ad of ultra-thin golden crepes rolled into tight cylinders, filled with rich, glossy chocolate-hazelnut spread. Crepes stacked casually on a rustic wooden board, elegant dark chocolate spiral stripes decorating each piece. Gooey chocolate peeks from the ends, lightly browned crispy edges visible. Slow, mouthwatering macro shots with smooth pans and tilts, highlighting the creamy filling, glossy chocolate, and soft texture. Warm natural daylight, vibrant green plant bokeh background, soft cinematic lighting. Subtle motion: chocolate drizzle falling in slow-motion, crepe stack slightly rotating, steam rising, inviting and indulgent. Hyper-realistic textures, professional styling, 8K resolution, perfectly framed for social media and ad storytelling.
```

## Joyful Woman at Prehistoric Mammoth Park

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A joyful young woman with long, voluminous wavy golden hair cascading over her shoulders, strands softly catching the sunlight, laughing openly with a carefree, radiant smile that shows pure happiness. She is sitting casually on an ancient weathered stone platform, surrounded by enormous carved stone woolly mammoth statues, their massive tusks and textured stone surfaces evoking a dramatic prehistoric atmosphere inside a sunlit paleontological park.
She wears a flowy {argument name="dress color" default="emerald green"} slip dress with a subtle leopard print pattern, lightweight fabric gently moving with the summer breeze, thin delicate straps resting naturally on her shoulders, the dress draping elegantly over her figure. On her feet are black platform sandals, modern and stylish, contrasting beautifully with the ancient surroundings. Beside her on the stone rests a black geometric chain bag, structured design with metallic chain details catching soft highlights.
Her posture is relaxed and natural, one hand resting behind her for balance, the other loosely placed on her knee, body slightly turned toward the light. Her expression is full of life—eyes slightly squinted from laughter, cheeks softly flushed, glowing skin with natural makeup, sun-kissed warmth.
The scene is captured during a sunny summer day at golden hour, with warm golden light wrapping around her and the statues, creating a dreamy glow. The background fades into a soft creamy bokeh, with hints of greenery, stone pathways, and distant sculptures blurred for depth. Colors are vibrant yet natural, rich greens, warm stone tones, and golden highlights perfectly balanced.
Shot in a high-end fashion photography style, cinematic composition, shallow depth of field, ultra-sharp subject focus with beautifully textured fabrics and skin detail. Highly detailed, photorealistic, realistic shadows and light falloff, organic film grain, subtle color shifts characteristic of Fujifilm Superia 400, nostalgic yet modern aesthetic, professional editorial quality, 8k realism, natural motion, candid lifestyle fashion moment.
```

## Editorial Fashion Contact Sheet in 3x3 Grid

> Minimal, stüdyo ışıklı moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Editorial fashion contact sheet of a modern woman with a sharp black bob haircut wearing white oval sunglasses, a beige tailored suit over a red sweater vest and white shirt, photographed in a minimalist studio with a neutral grey backdrop. Multiple frames arranged in a 3×3 grid showing varied angles—front, side, top-down, back view—while the subject repeatedly reaches her hand toward the camera lens, creating strong depth and perspective distortion. Clean high-fashion styling, bold color contrast, soft diffused studio lighting, crisp focus, contemporary magazine aesthetic, 35mm–50mm look, ultra-sharp, high resolution.
```

## Photorealistic Self-Portrait Drawing with Reference Images

> Fotogerçekçi portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Ultra-photorealistic editorial lifestyle photograph shot on a full-frame DSLR with an 85mm f/1.8 lens. Natural indoor daylight from the left. THE_SUBJECT (from attached reference) must be replicated with 100% identity fidelity, exact facial structure, skin tone, proportions, and natural texture.

THE_SUBJECT is seated on a couch, angled slightly toward camera, holding a large 12.9-inch iPad Pro vertically and showing the screen directly to the viewer. In the other hand, THE_SUBJECT holds an Apple Pencil naturally, as if paused mid-drawing.

Insert THE_SCREEN (attached reference image) exactly onto the iPad display with correct proportions and perspective. The screen should appear realistically illuminated with subtle glass reflections and edge glare. Both face and iPad screen in sharp focus. No stylization, no illustration effect; it must look like a real professional photograph.
```

The subject is wearing a structural, oversized crimson sun hat and a sharply tailored scarlet suit with a white silk bow blouse. The environment is incredibly dense and busy: floor-to-ceiling wooden shelves overflowing with dusty hat boxes, spools of unraveling thread, antique mannequin heads, scattered sewing needles, and rolls of decaying velvet fabric. SURREAL ELEMENT: The fabric of the subject's red suit appears to be physically 'bleeding' into the room, with long red silk ribbons extending from the blazer's hem and weaving through the surrounding clutter like living veins, suspending scissors and thimbles in mid-air. Lighting is dramatic Rembrandt style, with a single shaft of dusty light cutting through the haze to illuminate the subject's face and the vibrant red texture. Shot on a Hasselblad H6D-100c with an 80mm f/2.8 lens, creating a shallow depth of field that keeps the subject razor-sharp while the cluttered foreground blurs into textured bokeh. Kodak Ektar 100 film simulation for punchy, deep reds and organic grain. Tactile textures of dust, silk, and splintered wood.",

    "intent": "Editorial Fashion meets Magical Realism",

    "device_profile": "High-End Medium Format Photography"
  },
  "frame": {
    "aspect": "4:5",
    "composition": "Centralized Seated Figure amidst Chaos",
    "layout": "Golden Triangle",
    "camera_angle": "Eye-Level, slightly low to emphasize the hat",
    "tilt_roll_degrees": "0"
  },
  "subject": {
    "gender": "Female",

## Cinematic Low-Key Indoor Portrait

> Sinematik, minimal moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Cinematic, Low-key Indoor Portrait Framed In A Medium Side Profile, With The Subject Seated At A Wooden Desk Under A Single Warm Desk Lamp. The Pose Is Contemplative And Focused, Hands Loosely Clasped Over An Open Notebook, With The Gaze Directed Downward As If Deep In Thought. Lighting Is Controlled And Intimate, Using A Warm Tungsten Key Light (-2000k) From The Desk Lamp Positioned To The Left, Creating A Dramatic Chiaroscuro Effect: Soft Highlights On The Face, Hands, And Tabletop, And Deep Shadows Falling Across The Background And Clothing, The Background Is A Smooth, Dark {argument name="background color" default="Burgundy Tone (2a1416)"} That Absorbs Light And Enhances The Subject's Isolation In The Scene. A Minimal Set Of Props An Hourglass With Metallic Accents And A Closed Book Adds Subtle Texture And Storytelling Without Distracting From The Subject. Wardrobe Is Simple, Matte, And Monochrome, Such As A Dark Button-up Or Sweater That Minimizes Reflections And Emphasizes Sculpted Lighting On The Face. Lens Simulation Approximates An 85mm Full-frame At F/1.8, Producing A Shallow Depth Of Field With Crisp Subject Detail And A Velvety Blur In The Background, Texture Quality Highlights Fine Details In Skin, Hair, Fabric Fold, The Grain Of The Wooden Desk, And Warm Specular Highlights From The Lamp's Metal Components. The Overall Mood Is Introspective, Cinematic, And Atmospheric Blending Stillness, Warmth, And Narrative Tension In A Refined Fashion-editorial Style. Editorial, Modern, High-detail, Cinematic Composition, Ultra-realistic Textures, Professional Fashion Photography Style.
```

A slow, cinematic close-up video of crispy golden chicken nuggets. One nugget is bitten in half, revealing tender, juicy shredded white meat inside. The coating crunch subtly emphasized with slow-motion micro-detail shots, highlighting texture and golden crispiness. Professional food styling, hyper-realistic, appetizing, mouthwatering, [food], 4K, cinematic lighting, smooth camera movement.

## Avant-Garde Heartbeat Jewelry Advertisement

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Avant-garde romantic jewelry advertisement concept inspired by emotional connection and time-freeze. A matte blush-green cube opens like a secret vault, revealing a glowing rose-gold interior shaped with subtle heartbeat wave textures instead of glitter. Rose gold rings emerge as if paused mid-heartbeat, gently orbiting the box in an asymmetric rhythm — including abstract heart-line rings, tension-set diamond bands, and sculptural minimalist stacking rings. Instead of ribbons, translucent green silk light-trails flow like slow-motion sound waves, wrapping softly around the rings. Fine golden particles drift upward like stardust breathing in sync, mixed with faint rose mist. Cinematic lighting with soft rim highlights and emotional depth, shallow focus creating a dreamlike pause-in-time feeling. High-fashion jewelry campaign aesthetic, refined, modern, poetic. Brand names “HEARTSYNC” and “HEARTYNSNC” subtly engraved on different sides of the cube, minimal and intentional. Clean pastel background with gradient blush tones and atmospheric haze. Ultra-detailed, photorealistic with a conceptual surreal twist.
--ar 3:4 --stylize 280 --v 6 --q 2
```

Lighting: soft cinematic key light from front-left, gentle rim light, medium contrast, even exposure. Optics & camera feel: 85mm portrait lens equivalent, f/1.8–2.2 shallow depth of field, crisp focus on eyes, ISO 100, 1/200s. Color & finish: natural color grading, warm highlights, neutral midtones, restrained film grain, high dynamic range, ultra-detailed fabric and metal reflections. Composition: centered subject, headroom preserved, waist-up framing, cinematic editorial style. Resolution: ultra-realistic, high-detail, 8k output, photoreal.
Negative prompt:
watermarks, text, logos across frame, oversaturated colors, cartoon/anime style, extra limbs, heavy smoothing/plastic skin, motion blur, low-res, JPEG artifacts, incorrect logos or extra objects.
Use aspect ratio 4:5 for portrait framing.
```

## Sensual Luxury Chocolate Commercial Scene

> Sinematik, lüks görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A slow, sensual luxury chocolate commercial shot in ultra-high definition. The scene opens in near darkness as a premium espresso-brown gift box with soft gold accents slowly rotates on a reflective surface. A warm spotlight reveals the embossed title “{argument name="product name" default="SWEET PROMISE"}” in an elegant serif font, paired with a subtle botanical emblem. As the lid gently lifts in slow motion, molten milk chocolate surges upward in a gravity-defying burst, captured at 120fps for dramatic fluid motion. Glossy heart-shaped chocolates rise and orbit the splash, rotating gracefully at different depths, their surfaces catching highlights like polished jewels. Thick chocolate ribbons stretch, drip, and shatter into silky droplets mid-air while cocoa powder blooms softly like a golden mist. Cinematic mocha-and-gold color grading, shallow depth of field, creamy bokeh background, soft rim lighting outlining every chocolate curve. Macro close-ups transition into wide hero shots, emphasizing texture, gloss, and richness. The motion feels slow, seductive, and indulgent—pure luxury confectionery advertising, premium product film, elegant pacing, emotionally rich, visually irresistible.
```

    "Background Style": "Calm, uniform color blocks with subtle pigment texture"

    "Shadows": "Soft, subtle shadows cast directly beneath the subject, reinforcing the overhead view"
  },

      "No depth of field perspective"
    ],
    "Layout": "Graphic, editorial feel, simple geometry",

  },
  "Lighting": {
    "Type": "Soft studio lighting",
    "Light Direction": "Uniform top lighting",
    "Contrast": "Low to medium contrast",

}

## Cinematic studio portrait with exact facial match

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a 3:4 cinematic studio portrait of a young Indian man using the uploaded reference image as the only identity source, with 100% exact facial match and no changes to face, proportions, or features. He has short, messy black hair and a short beard, smiling confidently with arms crossed. He wears a teal long-sleeve crew-neck shirt. Three-quarter view, looking slightly upward. Pure black studio background. Dramatic red rim light from the right and cool blue fill light from the left, creating strong contrast and depth. High-end portrait photography, realistic skin texture, sharp focus, cinematic lighting, ultra-realistic look.
```

## Dramatic Cinematic Low-Angle Portrait Prompt (Reference Image Required)

> Sinematik, minimal mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a dramatic cinematic low-angle portrait of a character, with a strict guarantee that the face and hairstyle remain consistent with the reference image. The subject stands in a dark, minimalist space, illuminated by a single sharp beam of light cutting across the face and shoulders. The subject wears the same outfit as in the reference photo. The gaze is directed straight at the camera, forming a strong, almost heroic silhouette against the deep shadows around the subject. The background features an abandoned building. The contrast between light and darkness emphasizes the subject’s steady posture and confident presence. The scene is captured with high-contrast lighting, subtle film-grain texture, and a moody, atmospheric tone.
```

## High-Fashion Editorial Photoshoot Prompt (Three Women)

> Sinematik, minimal moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
"A high-fashion editorial photoshoot featuring three young women posing confidently against a raw concrete wall in an industrial studio setting. They wear matching black, form-fitting bodysuits with structured bust detailing and thin straps, styled in a sleek, minimalist aesthetic. Each model accessorizes with bold silver jewelry, including chunky chain necklaces with metallic nameplate pendants, diamond bracelets, and rings.
Two women stand upright with strong, assertive poses—hands on hips or resting against the wall—while one woman crouches or kneels in the foreground, creating dynamic triangular compositions. Their expressions are serious, confident, and unapologetic, with direct eye contact toward the camera.
Footwear consists of dramatic black knee-high boots with thick, furry textures, adding contrast and visual weight to the outfits. Hair styling varies: one with long dark wavy hair, one with soft medium-length brown waves, and one with long natural red hair, all styled loosely for an effortless editorial feel. Makeup is polished and natural with subtle contouring and neutral tones.
Lighting is soft but directional, highlighting skin texture and sculpting the body while maintaining a moody, cinematic tone. The color palette is restrained—black outfits against muted gray concrete—emphasizing contrast, attitude, and modern luxury. Shot in ultra-high resolution, fashion magazine style, sharp focus, professional studio photography."
```

Cute young blonde girl taking a mirror selfie, ultra-detailed photorealistic portrait, platinum blonde hair styled in a soft loose bun with slightly messy elegance, delicate face-framing strands, wispy baby hairs around the forehead, natural volume and silky texture. Bright big expressive eyes with a glassy sparkle, long curled lashes, softly defined brows, smooth porcelain skin with visible natural texture, subtle rosy blush on cheeks, pink glossy lips with a wet shine, gentle cupid’s bow, warm innocent smile radiating happiness and positivity.

She is wearing a stylish argyle cropped vest sweater with a finely knitted texture, pastel pink and deep black diamond pattern, V-neckline showing the collarbones, cozy yet fashionable fit. A delicate heart-shaped necklace rests naturally on her chest, catching soft light. Holding a smartphone in one hand with a pastel pink phone case, mirror selfie angle, fingers relaxed. A small elegant pink shoulder bag hangs from her arm, matching the overall color palette. Paired with a clean white high-waisted skirt, smooth fabric, feminine and minimal, enhancing a soft cute aesthetic.

Scene set in a cozy aesthetic bedroom, neutral cream and beige tones, minimal decor, soft bedding, tidy background. Natural daylight streaming through a window, sheer white curtains glowing, warm airy atmosphere, soft shadows on the walls. Cinematic natural lighting, balanced exposure, shallow depth of field, background softly blurred. Ultra-sharp focus on face and outfit, highly detailed fabric knit, realistic skin pores, natural proportions. Fashion influencer mirror selfie style, modern cute aesthetic, joyful happy vibe, high resolution, professional photography quality, dreamy yet realistic look.

## Alpine Snow Portrait with Trapper Hat

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik manzara üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Image should be an ultra-high-resolution, close-up, ultra-photorealistic photograph in 8K quality. It is taken with a medium format Hasselblad H6D-400c digital camera and a 100mm f/2.8 studio portrait lens, resulting in flawless sharpness, extreme detail, and a cinematic depth of field.

The person in the photo is wearing a {argument name="hat type" default="white faux-fur trapper hat"}, an oversized, voluminous dark red sweatshirt, oversized black pants, and bulky black ski boots. Their hair is black, wet, and curly, reaching to the chin. The hairstyle must match the attached first photo exactly, without any distortion.

The camera is set at knee height in front of the person, with a slight low-angle tilt to create an intimate effect. The person is sitting on the edge of a small snow slope or a low wooden platform (snow deck) that is slightly elevated above the surrounding snow.

In terms of pose, their knees are pointed toward the camera, their elbows rest on their knees, and their hands are relaxed. Their head is slightly tilted.

The background consists of bright, snow-covered alpine slopes with a clear, deep blue sky and distant mountains. The scenery surrounds the subject, and there are no ski lifts, crowds, fences, or other foreign objects in the main snow area (though some irrelevant snow tracks may appear). In the foreground, the snow is in focus, creating a silhouette effect that slightly blurs the view of the boots and snow but does not obscure the eyes and face.

The emphasis is on the details and textures of the face, skin, and clothing.
```

## Cinematic Portrait of Three Actresses in Wet Look Aesthetic

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
"A hyper-realistic cinematic portrait captures three prominent actresses Sydney Sweeney, Sadie Sink, and Ana de Armas standing side-by-side in a sophisticated, minimalist architectural space with muted grey concrete walls. Sydney, on the left, wears a cream-colored silk corset gown with sheer paneling; Sadie, in the center, dons a vibrant scarlet red one-shoulder dress with geometric cutouts; and Ana, on the right, faces away to showcase a backless black lace evening gown. All three women sport a coordinated "wet look" aesthetic with damp, slicked-back hair and luminous, dewy skin that catches the soft, directional overhead lighting. The composition is intimate and powerful, utilizing a shallow depth of field and a moody, high-fashion color palette to emphasize the intricate textures of the fabric and the striking gaze of the subjects."
```

## Luxury Dessert Still Life with Diamonds and Molten Center

```
Dessert Name: {argument name="Dessert Name" default="<YOUR DESSERT NAME HERE>"}

A hyper-realistic luxury dessert still life inspired by the dessert named "{argument name="Dessert Name 2" default="<YOUR DESSERT NAME HERE>"}". The dessert’s form and structure accurately match the real-world dessert implied by the name (cake, tart, mousse, pastry, soufflé, chocolate dessert, parfait, custard, etc.), presented as an elegant, indulgent centerpiece with a refined pink-toned luxury palette.

If the dessert implies a molten center, lava, glaze, or soft interior, the molten element is abundant, thick, glossy, and slow-flowing, emerging generously with smooth ribbons and slight pooling, clean and appetizing rather than messy.

The dessert is displayed in an ornate vintage crystal dessert goblet or dish appropriate to its form, with faceted crystal details and a solid, elegant base.

The upper surface is lightly dusted with ultra-fine diamond-like glitter, behaving purely as delicate optical sparkle, extremely fine, naturally scattered, with no visible grains and no interaction with liquids.

Diamonds are arranged in a clear hierarchy: several oversized hero diamonds, many medium-sized supporting diamonds and gemstones, and subtle micro sparkle. Size contrast is strong and intentional, creating depth and richness without uniformity.

All diamonds and gemstones sit dry and clean on the dessert surface, completely separate from any lava, glaze, cream, or syrup. Each stone has sharp brilliant-cut facets, strong internal refraction, high refractive index, and crystal-clear transparency. Only 3–5 stones emit controlled starburst flares; all others remain softly reflective.

All molten elements, glazes, syrups, or creams remain pure and uninterrupted, with no solid inclusions. Garnishes implied by the dessert name appear naturally and refined.

Centered symmetrical hero composition, slight low-angle view, seamless dark red to black gradient background, high-jewelry advertising lighting, ultra-detailed, cinematic realism, premium beauty commercial aesthetic.
```

## High-Fashion Editorial in Anti-Gravity Color Box

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
[INPUT IMAGE: USER_PHOTO] Use the person in the input image as the ONLY subject. Preserve their identity and facial features clearly.

Create a hyper-realistic high-fashion editorial photo inside a surreal 3D geometric “color box” room (a hollow cube / tilted cube set). Each render MUST randomly choose:
1) a bold single-color box (monochrome environment, vivid and saturated),
2) a dynamic “cool” fashion pose (gravity-defying or extreme stretch / leap / sideways bracing against the walls),
3) a dramatic camera angle (wide-angle 24–35mm equivalent, tilted horizon, strong perspective).

The subject appears full-body and sharp, wearing an avant-garde fashion styling that feels modern and editorial (clean silhouette, stylish layering, premium fabric texture). Keep clothing tasteful and fashion-forward. The subject’s pose should feel athletic, stylish, and unusual—like a magazine campaign shot.

Lighting: studio quality, crisp and cinematic; strong key light with controlled soft shadows, subtle rim light; realistic reflections and bounce light from the colored walls. Ultra-detailed skin texture, natural pores, realistic fabric weave, clean edges, high dynamic range. 
Composition: subject centered with plenty of negative space and strong geometric lines; the box color is a SINGLE bold color and MUST be different each run (random vivid hue). The subject’s outfit contrasts well with the box color.

Output: hyper-real, photorealistic, 8k detail, editorial campaign quality, sharp focus on subject, no motion blur, no distortion of face, natural proportions.
```

## High-Fidelity Portrait with Extreme Identity Mapping and Technical Rib-Knit Texture

> Fotogerçekçi mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
[Header] (9:16)
Raw high-fidelity photograph, iPhone 17 Pro sensor simulation, indoor ambient lighting with soft front-fill flash, zero bokeh, SHARP focus from the foreground subject to the background tiled wall, f/5.6 aperture for structural depth, authentic digital grain.

[Subject & Identity Inheritance]
Identity Mapping: Absolute physical inheritance from Reference Image A. The generated subject must inherit the exact facial geometry, specific eye shape, lip volume, chin structure, and all original hair characteristics (texture, length, volume, and part) directly from Image A without manual descriptor intervention.
Bio-Fidelity: Detailed skin physics including micro-pores, satin-finish hydration, and visible fine vellus hair along the jawline. High-definition rendering of the shoulders and neck architecture.
Expression: Magnetic presence; inheriting the self-assured gaze and facial resting-state directly from Image A, looking back over the shoulder.
[Outfit & Physicality]
Technical Description: High-impact technical rib-knit body-conscious set in muted {argument name="outfit color" default="earth-tone"} horizontal stripes (terracotta, tan, and beige).
Textile Geometry: Off-the-shoulder neckline architecture with full-length compression-fit sleeves featuring technical distressed/frayed cuff detailing.
Surface Physics: High-tensile material-to-skin surface compression. The textile must exhibit realistic physical tension lines as it contours the anatomical gluteal proportions, pelvic symmetry, and an emphasized waist-to-hip ratio.
Physicality: Athletic-to-lean build; extreme torso torsion and symmetrical proximal limb geometry.
[Pose & Composition]
Geometry: Extreme S-curve torsion pose. The subject is standing in a rear-profile perspective, rotating her upper torso 180 degrees to face the camera directly.
Interaction: Left hand is subtly raised, fingers interacting with a hair strand near the shoulder, showing realistic finger-joint articulation and skin texture.
Framing: Medium shot, vertical focus. The subject is positioned on the left side of the frame, angled to lead the viewer’s eye toward the background hallway.
[Environment & Lighting]
Atmosphere: Modern domestic or hotel interior hallway.
Interior Details: Foreground features a high-detail tiled wall with horizontal tan/beige rectangular porcelain tiles and precise grout lines. Background includes a dark-wood door with a white technical signage card. Neutral grey stone-tile flooring with realistic specular reflections.
Lighting: Diffused 3000K warm indoor light supplemented by a soft front-fill flash. This creates brilliant illumination on the subject’s skin and the rib-knit texture, while casting clean, defined shadows on the tiled background.
[Failure Prevention Rule]
Command: "Identity Mapping: Inherit all facial and hair traits from Reference Image A onto the subject." Use "Technical rib-knit body-conscious set" and "Anatomical gluteal proportions" to ensure high-fidelity
```

## Cinematic Lakeside Fashion Portrait Prompt (Golden Hour)

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Ultra-detailed cinematic portrait of a beautiful adult blonde woman standing by a tranquil lake at sunset, captured in a high-end fashion photography style. She has very long, voluminous wavy blonde hair flowing freely down her back and over her shoulders, individual strands softly lifted by a gentle evening breeze, hair glowing subtly with rim light from the setting sun. Her bright blue eyes are intense and expressive, looking directly into the camera with a confident, alluring, seductive gaze, slightly parted lips with natural rosy tone, refined facial features, smooth realistic skin texture with visible pores, soft peachy blush, subtle highlighter on cheekbones, natural lashes, minimal elegant makeup.

She is wearing a white cropped hoodie, clean and modern, slightly fitted to accentuate her waist, soft cotton fabric with realistic folds and shadows, paired with tight white leggings that contour her long legs and hips, matte fabric with gentle stretch highlights, minimalist sporty-chic aesthetic. Her pose is relaxed yet sensual: body angled three-quarters toward the camera, weight shifted to one leg, one hand lifted into her hair, fingers loosely tangled in the waves, the other arm resting naturally by her side. Her posture conveys confidence, femininity, and calm self-assurance.

The setting is a peaceful lakeside with smooth reflective water stretching into the distance. The background features a dramatic cloudy sunset sky, thick cinematic clouds layered across the horizon, painted with deep blues, smoky purples, muted violets, soft pinks, and warm golden-orange highlights from the sinking sun. The colors of the sky are mirrored on the lake’s surface, creating a symmetrical, dreamy reflection. Distant shoreline silhouettes and faint trees are softly blurred, adding depth without distracting from the subject.

Lighting is cinematic and photorealistic, golden-hour light transitioning into blue hour, warm highlights kissing her face and hair, cool ambient shadows balancing the scene, subtle backlighting creating a glowing outline around her hair and shoulders. Shallow depth of field with creamy bokeh, professional camera look, shot on a full-frame DSLR or mirrorless camera, 85mm lens, f/1.8, HDR, perfect exposure, cinematic color grading, soft contrast, rich dynamic range.

Mood is serene, sensual, and cinematic, a blend of calm nature and modern fashion elegance. Ultra-high resolution, hyper-realistic details, natural proportions, no distortion, sharp focus on eyes, luxury lifestyle editorial vibe, dramatic yet refined composition, film-still aesthetic, 8K quality, photorealism at its finest.
```

Lifestyle collage of unevenly sized images, modern Instagram / Pinterest aesthetic, fitness and beach-inspired theme.

The composition features multiple photos arranged asymmetrically, with different image sizes and aspect ratios placed together in a casual, editorial collage layout. No strict grid alignment. Natural overlaps and varied spacing create an organic, curated look.

Image Content (Across the Collage)

Adult women with athletic, toned physiques, photographed in different environments

Beach scenes at sunset with ocean waves and warm golden light

Outdoor tropical setting with palm trees and poolside vibes

Indoor mirror selfie with casual fitness wear

Back-view and side-profile poses emphasizing posture and body lines

Activewear, bikinis, and fitness outfits in neutral, black, and muted tones

Subjects are confident, relaxed, and stylish, captured in candid or lightly posed moments. Faces are partially obscured or turned away, creating a lifestyle/editorial feel rather than portrait focus.

Color & Lighting

Warm highlights mixed with soft shadows
Natural daylight and sunset tones
Earthy neutrals, skin tones, muted blacks and greens
Consistent cinematic color grading across all images

Visual Style

Instagram lifestyle collage
Fitness and wellness aesthetic
Editorial mood
Candid, natural photography
High realism
Soft contrast

Camera & Composition

Mix of full-body, mid-shot, and close-up framing
Varied perspectives back
Natural depth of field
No strict symmetry
Casual magazine-style layout

Mood Keywords

Confident
Natural
Aesthetic
Summer lifestyle
Fitness-inspired
Relaxed
Editorial

Negative Prompt

Perfect grid layout, equal image sizes, heavy borders, text overlays, logos, watermarks, exaggerated body proportions, oversexualization, CGI look, cartoon style, harsh lighting
```

[Pose & Composition]

Framing: Vertical 9:16 orientation. The subject holds a dark technical smartphone centrally at face level, capturing the reflection in a full-length mirror.
Anatomy: Realistic shoulder-joint displacement and upper-body symmetry, reflecting the athletic-to-lean transition characteristic of Image A.
[Environment & Lighting]

Atmosphere: Minimalist domestic interior (bedroom).
Interior Details: The subject is seated on a white bed with high-detail quilted linens and realistic fabric folds. The background is a clean, neutral-toned flat-painted wall.
Lighting: Diffused {argument name="lighting temperature" default="3500K warm"} indoor lighting. The light source provides even illumination across the skin and the black textile, creating soft shadows that define the muscle-to-skin interaction in the legs.
Background Integrity: All background architectural elements and bedding textures must remain in sharp focus with zero artificial blurring.
[Failure Prevention Rule]

Command: Use "Technical off-the-shoulder micro-romper" and "Full-length compression-fit sleeves" to ensure the AI maintains the exact garment fit.
Constraint: Explicitly command "Zero artificial bokeh" and "Sharp focus on bedding texture" to ensure the photo maintains an authentic smartphone aesthetic. Specify "Anatomical bust vo
```

## Cinematic Automotive Triptych Portrait Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Transform this image [UPLOAD YOUR IMAGE] into a 64K DSLR shot resolution of a ultra-hyperrealistic gritty, cinematic triptych-style portrait featuring a modern urban automotive lifestyle scene. The image is divided into three horizontal frames stacked vertically, each telling a different part of the story.
Top frame: An intense close-up of a man’s face (same face as the uploaded image) reflected in a car’s side mirror.

His expression is focused, calm, and determined. Soft natural light highlights skin texture, subtle stubble, and sharp facial features. The shallow depth of field blurs the background, creating a cinematic, introspective mood.

Middle frame: A full-body shot of the same man

leaning casually against a sleek black muscle car (Ferrari-style). He stands confidently in an industrial alleyway surrounded by concrete walls and urban architecture.

Outfit: fitted black graphic t-shirt, slim gray trousers, brown low-top sneakers. Hands relaxed in pockets, posture confident but effortless. The car's glossy black paint reflects the surroundings, emphasizing power and elegance. Low-angle perspective adds dominance and strength.

Bottom frame: A dramatic rear shot of the

car, taillights glowing softly. A custom license plate is centered, adding authenticity and personality. The alley fades into darkness, with leading lines drawing attention to the vehicle's aggressive stance.
Environment & Mood: Urban, industrial alley, concrete textures, muted tones.

Mood: confidence, ambition, independence, modern masculinity.

Atmosphere feels like a high-end automotive editorial or cinematic lifestyle campaign.

Style & Technical Details:-

Ultra-hyperrealistic photography

Cinematic color grading (cool shadows, neutral highlights)

High contrast with controlled blacks

Shallow depth of field

Soft natural lighting mixed with ambient

city light

Subtle film grain Sharp focus on subject, background slightly

desaturated

Camera & Quality:

Shot on full-frame DSLR / mirrorless

35mm and 85mm lens combination

Low ISO, crisp details

8K ultra-high resolution

Professional automotive photography, editorial

quality. Octane render, Ultra-hyperrealistic &

Unreal Engine 5.
```

Character wears {argument name="clothing description" default="a dark blue lightweight shirt, off-white trousers, and is barefoot"} to show raw texture.

Large area soft light from the front right, silver reflector on the left, subtle rim light from the top.

4. Partially obscured portrait
5. Close-up of hand covering the face
6. Composition with hair covering the eyes
7. Detail of hand touching the chin
8. Half-body side sitting posture
9. Cinematic side profile with tears
```

{

Chilly air, soft mist in the distance, wet stone ground with subtle reflections. Fairy chimneys and valley silhouettes in the background with gentle haze. Overcast sky with a slight warm break in the clouds near sunrise.\
\
BACKGROUND BALLOONS: multiple hot air balloons floating in the mid-distance sky behind her (small-to-medium size, not too close), softly blurred by depth of field, some partially fading into mist for realism. Balloons must NOT overlap or block her head/face.\
\
OUTFIT: beige wool coat, chunky knit scarf, knit beanie, simple gloves. Natural minimal makeup.\
\
ACTION: holding a warm drink cup (salep/coffee) with both hands near chest, relaxed shoulders, gentle friendly smile, warm eye contact.\
\
CAMERA: eye-level, medium close-up (chest-up), 50–85mm portrait look, shallow depth of field, sharp focus on eyes, clean influencer photo realism, subtle film grain.\
\
DETAILS: a few subtle raindrops on coat/hair (very minimal), cozy welcoming travel vibe.",

}

## Editorial Street-Fashion Portrait Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a hyper-realistic DSLR cinematic full-body portrait of a stylish young man with sharp, well-defined facial features and a confident, dynamic pose. He is captured in natural yet dramatic lighting with cinematic depth of field, crisp focus, and professional DSLR clarity. He wears an oversized distressed dark brown and beige patchwork knit sweater with contrasting thick stitching, intentional worn holes, and long hanging cords or drawstrings for a raw, avant-garde look, paired with black relaxed-fit cargo pants. He has black over-ear “gloomyboy” style headphones with a clearly visible wired connection and a minimal metal ring on his hand. Editorial street-fashion aesthetic with ultra-detailed fabric textures, realistic skin tones, subtle film grain, cinematic color grading, shallow depth of field, high contrast, and a moody yet stylish atmosphere.
```

Transform the {argument name="brand name" default="[BRAND NAME]"} logo into a hyper-realistic, 3D fluffy object. Keep the original shape and exact brand colors. Cover the entire surface in soft, detailed fur with a realistic hair texture. Use cinematic lighting to create subtle backlighting and soft shadows, making the logo appear tactile and surreal. Place the logo in the center of a clean black background, floating gently with a modern, stylish look.
The style should feel cozy, playful, and visually striking. Render in ultra-high 4K resolution with photorealistic quality.
```

    },

  "physics_simulation": {

  "lighting": {

    "shadows": "soft directional shadows under chin and muscles",
    "highlights": "specular highlights on sweaty skin"
  },

  "image_style": {

    "processing": "minimal post-processing, realistic tone mapping",
    "grain": "subtle fine grain for realism",
    "focus": "slight background blur, subject sharp",
    "color_grading": "cool neutral tones, high contrast fitness look"
  },

  "composition": {
    "rule_of_thirds": true,
    "depth_of_field": "shallow",
    "motion_feel": "captured mid-action, dynamic energy"
  },

  "negative_prompt": [

    "plastic skin",
    "AI artifacts",
    "extra limbs",
    "distorted face",
    "
  ]
}

## Pose Change with Fixed Reference Prompt

The setting remains an outdoor rose garden filled with vibrant {argument name="rose color" default="red"} roses under soft natural daylight and a clear blue sky. The subject is wearing the same white lace halter-neck outfit, white gloves, white lace stockings, and holding the same wicker basket filled with red roses. The only change is the pose: – Replace the original pose with

## High-Fidelity Fitness Portrait Prompt (Identity Locking)

> Minimal, stüdyo ışıklı mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
[Header] (9:16)
Raw high-fidelity photograph, iPhone 17 Pro sensor simulation, professional studio lighting, zero bokeh, SHARP focus from the foreground lavender mat to the background topographic wall panels, f/8 aperture for absolute structural depth, subtle digital grain.

[Subject & Identity Locking]
Identity: Absolute preservation of Image A’s specific facial geometry, emphasizing the almond eye shape, high lip volume, and defined chin structure.
Hair Physics: The hair must exhibit high-definition strand separation, cascading naturally down the back and over the shoulders, reflecting the warm amber backlighting.
Bio-Fidelity: Detailed skin physics including micro-pores, satin-finish hydration, and visible fine vellus hair along the jawline. Precise anatomical rendering of the hands resting on the furniture.
Expression: Magnetic presence; a self-assured, wide smile with high-saturation ruby-red lipstick, directed toward the camera lens.
[Outfit & Physicality]
Technical Description: High-impact technical marl-grey performance-wear set.
Upper Geometry: Long-sleeved compression-fit cropped top with a minimalist crew-neck architecture.
Lower Geometry: Matching high-waisted performance leggings featuring technical shaded textile architecture (contouring lines) that emphasize the gluteal proportions and lower-body silhouette.
Surface Physics: High-tensile material-to-skin surface compression. The fabric must exhibit realistic physical tension lines and micro-knit textures, specifically where it contours the gluteal proportions and proximal limb geometry.
Physicality: Confident self-assured kneeling pose with extreme torso torsion (S-curve). High-fidelity rendering of the anatomical waist-to-hip ratio and symmetrical muscle-to-skin interaction.
[Pose & Composition]
Geometry: High-impact torsion pose. The subject is kneeling on her left knee on a {argument name="mat color" default="lavender"} technical foam mat, leaning her upper body against a neutral-toned bouclé-textured sofa.
Framing: Medium full-shot. The composition is angled to highlight the posterior silhouette and the athletic-to-lean transition characteristic of Image A.
Anatomy: Realistic shoulder-joint displacement as she leans; zero AI distortion in the spinal curve.
[Environment & Lighting]
Atmosphere: Modern minimalist fitness studio or high-end domestic gym.
Interior Details: Lavender-colored technical foam yoga mat on a light-oak hardwood floor. The background features a large topographic abstract wall panel with 3D-contoured textures.
Lighting: Dual-source lighting configuration. A cool-toned front light ensures clarity on the face and textile texture, while a warm amber-to-pink LED backlight creates a glow along the topographic wall and the subject’s silhouette.
Background Integrity: All background architectural elements must remain in sharp focus with zero artificial blurring to maintain a professional photographic aesthetic.
[Failure Prevention Rule]
Co
```

## 2x2 Wellness and Fitness Lifestyle Collage Prompt

> Minimal portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
2×2 grid lifestyle collage with a clean fitness and wellness aesthetic, modern social-media style, soft neutral tones.

Top Left Panel – Workout Essentials, ar 5:8

Flat-lay composition of minimalist workout essentials on a dark yoga mat: white sneakers partially visible, white wireless earbuds with case, and two small white dumbbells. Clean, monochrome color palette. Soft natural lighting, tidy arrangement, calm fitness vibe.

Top Right Panel – Fitness Mirror Selfie ar 6:8

A sensual, blond, beautiful adult woman taking a mirror selfie in a modern kitchen or apartment. She wears a white tank top with deep neckline and light gray fitted shorts, athletic and toned physique. One hand holds a drink, the other holds the phone. Natural daylight, casual confident posture, wellness lifestyle aesthetic.

Bottom Left Panel – Healthy Breakfast Bowl 3:8

Overhead shot of a healthy yogurt bowl arranged neatly with sliced strawberries, blueberries, banana slices, granola, and pink yogurt base. Bright, fresh colors, clean presentation, soft natural light. Emphasis on nutrition and balance.

Bottom Right Panel – Hydration Tracker 2:8

Close-up of a clear reusable water bottle with time markers printed on the side, held by a woman’s hand. Minimal background, clean composition. Highlights hydration, routine, and self-care.

All four images feel cohesive through neutral tones, natural light, minimalist styling, and a calm wellness-focused mood.

Visual Style

Instagram lifestyle aesthetic
Clean and minimal
Wellness and fitness inspired
Soft natural lighting
High realism
Editorial social-media look

Camera & Composition

Mix of flat-lay, mirror selfie, overhead food shot, and close-up
Balanced framing
Sharp focus
Consistent color grading
No motion blur

Mood Keywords

Healthy
Balanced
Motivational
Clean
Modern
Self-care
Daily routine

Negative Prompt

Text overlays, emojis, cluttered backgrounds, harsh lighting, oversaturation, exaggerated body proportions, CGI look, cartoon style, watermarks, logos

## Cinematic Fashion Photo with Motion Blur Prompt

> Sinematik, minimal moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A cinematic fashion photograph of a confident young woman standing still while vintage cars move rapidly around her, creating strong motion blur streaks. The subject is sharply in focus, hands casually in her pockets, with a calm, introspective expression.
She is wearing a loose, slightly oversized button-down shirt in a soft muted blue or warm beige tone, tucked into high-waisted tailored trousers in a deep charcoal or dark brown shade. The outfit feels timeless, minimal, and elegant, inspired by classic editorial fashion.
Her hair is styled naturally with a few loose strands framing her face, subtle makeup with natural skin tones and defined lips.
The scene has a golden hour color grading, warm sunlight, soft shadows, and a nostalgic cinematic look. The background features vintage cars in motion, blurred with long-exposure photography, creating a sense of speed and contrast against the still subject.
Shallow depth of field, film grain texture, editorial photography style, high realism, dramatic lighting, atmospheric mood, 35mm lens look, fashion magazine aesthetic.
```

PROMPT FOR IMAGE 1: THE PINK MEHARI

> Prompt: A high-fashion editorial shot of a stunning brunette model with gold hoop earrings, sitting on the back of a vintage pastel pink Citroën Méhari. She is wearing a bold red and white striped bikini top with matching high-waisted wide-leg trousers featuring a floral overlay. The back of the open-air vehicle is overflowing with fresh pineapples. The background features tall beach grass and a hazy tropical sun. The style is 1970s Slim Aarons photography meets modern pop art, high saturation, vibrant pinks and greens, 8k resolution, cinematic lighting.
> 

PROMPT FOR IMAGE 2: The SURREAL POOL PARTY 

> Prompt: A surrealist fashion photograph of a woman in an orange ribbed one-piece swimsuit leaning against the pink edge of a bright turquoise swimming pool. In front of her is a cluttered, maximalist feast including a shrimp cocktail, a whole crab, a can of Heinz beans, a classic Nintendo GameBoy, a papaya, and a pink martini. She is holding a cigarette with a moody expression. The color palette is hyper-saturated with electric pinks, oranges, and teals. Flash photography style, grainy film aesthetic, whimsical yet edgy editorial vibe.
> 

KEY STYLE KEYWORDS FOR THESE IMAGES:

If you want to create more in this specific style, use these "power words":
 * Aesthetic: "Maximalist Surrealism," "Summer Pop Art," "High-Saturated Editorial."
 * Photography Style: "Flash photography," "Kodachrome film," "Slim Aarons inspired."
 * Colors: "Vibrant Fuchsia," "Tropical Teal," "Electric Orange."

## Alexander McQueen Sneaker Commercial Grid Prompt (3x3)

> Minimal, lüks logo üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a 3x3 grid for a high-end commercial marketing campaign, centered around Alexander McQueen men's luxury sneakers.

Each frame should present a unique visual concept while maintaining perfect product consistency across all nine images.

Grid Concepts (one per cell):

1. 'Iconic Hero Shot': Eye-level side view on a dark, glossy pedestal, emphasizing the signature oversized sole.

2. 'Extreme Macro Detail': Close-up of the heel or stitching, highlighting the top-tier leather texture and the 'Alexander McQueen' logo embossing.

3. 'Dynamic Interaction': The shoe stepping onto a surface, creating a subtle and elegant splash effect of black liquid or marble powder.

4. 'Minimalist Sculptural Feel': The shoe artistically balanced on a geometric concrete or marble block, emphasizing the architectural design.

5. 'Floating Lightness': The shoe suspended in the air with dynamic hanging laces, conveying supreme comfort and lightness.

6. 'Sensory Texture': A tactile atmosphere shot focusing on the contrast between the velvet or smooth leather upper and a rough stone texture.

7. 'Monochromatic Luxury': A color-block scene where the background perfectly matches the shoe's accent color, creating a bold editorial look.

8. 'Deconstructed Craftsmanship': An abstract arrangement of raw materials (leather swatches, rubber textures) surrounding the shoe, symbolizing high-quality construction.

9. 'Surreal Fusion': The shoe integrated into a surreal, dreamlike landscape, blending realism with high fashion.

Visual Rules:
* 'Subject': Alexander McQueen men's oversized sneakers.
* 'Consistency': The shoe's shape, proportion, sole thickness, logo placement, and typography must be 100% accurate.
* 'No Distortion': No deformation of the signature sole or branding.
* 'Separation': Clear separation between the shoe and the background.

Lighting and Style:
* Soft, controlled studio lighting with dramatic rim light to highlight the shoe's silhouette.
* Subtle highlights on the leather, deep realistic shadows under the sole.
* High dynamic range, 8K resolution, ultra-sharp focus.
* Editorial luxury fashion advertising aesthetic (Vogue/GQ style).
* Color Palette: Minimalist, monochromatic, metallic, and deep matte tones.

Overall Vibe:
* Modern, sophisticated, visually coherent.
* High-end commercial campaign for a luxury fashion brand.
* Surreal, cinematic, refined, and aspirational.
```

      "White wall radiator visible in background",
      "Plain neutral beige/off-white walls",
      "Beige lampshade in the upper corner"

  },

}
